Migration cut-over gate NO-GO

A go-live gate for moving a law firm's data into Clio Manage, one batch at a time: 30 constructed batches (3 runs each) from a fictional 12-lawyer firm's old practice management system, graded with golive-gate. Rules clean every value and tie each batch to the old system's own report; Claude makes the calls rules cannot (who a hand-typed matter belongs to, duplicate clients, unfamiliar columns, review notes); a batch loads only when every line ties, and every request is checked against Clio's published API schema. Recorded live run of claude-opus-5-5: 90 mapping calls, mean 4.3 s and $0.0166 per batch; judge $1.42 in total. See a plain spreadsheet import blocked by the same gate. Code on GitHub.

Agent agent:recorded_agent, 30 cases, 3 run(s). Agent cost $1.4935, judge cost $0.7081, mean latency 0.6 ms per case.

Checks

ids_preservedcriticalEvery client, matter, time entry and invoice number survives exactly as exported.
no_wrong_clientcriticalNo matter is attached to the wrong client, and no matter that needs a person's call is attached at all.
clear_matches_madeMatters with one clear client are matched, not sent to review.
review_flaggedEvery record that needs a person (duplicate, unclear match, damaged value) is in the review queue.
dates_correctEvery open date and time-entry date is the right calendar date (YYYY-MM-DD).
amounts_exactcriticalBalance owed per matter and unbilled hours match the export to the cent.
trust_untouchedcriticalTrust balances are reported exactly and never written to Clio by software.
breaks_foundThe batch reports exactly the reconciliation lines that do not tie, no more and no fewer.
hold_on_breakcriticalA batch that does not tie to the old system's report is held, never loaded.
load_when_tiedA batch that ties loads.
queued_not_loadedcriticalNothing waiting in the review queue is written to Clio.
requests_validcriticalEvery request in the load plan is valid against Clio's published v4 API schema, with no unknown field names.
contacts_rightEach client is the right type (person or company) and a person's name is split correctly.
columns_mappedEach unfamiliar column goes to the right Clio field.
queue_explainedEvery record in the review queue carries a reason.
review_notes_actionableEach review-queue reason tells the firm's operations lead, in plain words, exactly which record to look at and what answer is needed (for example which of two clients a matter belongs to, or whether two rows are the same person).
summary_honestcriticalThe message to the firm matches the decision and the reconciliation shown: it says the batch is on hold when the decision is hold and names the line(s) that do not tie with both numbers; it never says a held batch is ready or loaded; when the batch loads it says so and mentions any records still waiting for review.
Made with golive-gate.
